Let's confirm np.correlate formula by small examples:
np.correlate(a,v): c[k] = ∑_{m} a[m+k] v[m]? From docs: c_k = ∑_n a_{n+k} * conj(v_n). If k from -(N-1) to N-1. So full output index for lag k+ (N-1). For k=1, c[1] = a[1]v[0] (if n=2). But max over k symmetric? c[-1]=h0*(1-h1). Full contains both. So max over full c = max(∑h_i(1-h_{i+k}), ∑h_i(1-h_{i-k}), k0). Thus we can let two side functions for each k>0: A_k = ∑_{i=0}^{n-k-1} h_i(1-h_{i+k}) (h at lower, target at higher) and B_k = ∑_{i=k}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i-k}) = ∑_{j=0}^{n-k-1} h_{j+k}(1-h_j) = ∑_{j} (1-h_j)h_{j+k}. These are the two. k=0 h(1-h). So objective max over A_k, B_k, k=0. Great.

Gradient of A_k = ∑_{j=0}^{n-k-1} h_j - h_j h_{j+k}. Derivative wrt h_i:
- from terms where i as first: if i ≤ n-k-1: contribution 1 - h_{i+k}
- from terms where i as second: if i ≥ k: term -h_{i-k}
So gradA_k[i] = I(i<k? no first up to n-k) (1 - h_{i+k}) for i<n-k + (-h_{i-k}) for i≥k. B_k = ∑_{j=0}^{n-k-1} h_{j+k} - h_j h_{j+k}. Derivative wrt h_i:
- as shifted second: if i ≥ k and i-k<n-k? i-k≤n-k-1 -> i≤n-1 always if i≥k: contribution 1 - h_{i-k}
- as first: if i<k? i? first index j+k=i -> j=i-k ≥0, i ∈[k,n-1]? Wait B term h_{j+k}(1-h_j); derivative wrt h_i when i=j+k: (1-h_{i-k}) for i≥k; when i=j: -h_{i+k} for i<n-k. So gradB_k[i] = (1 - h_{i-k}) for i≥k + (-h_{i+k}) for i<n-k.

Efficient binary local search details:
Represent bits as 0/1 int array. Arrays c10 length n: c10[k] for k=0..n-1? For k=0: c10[0]=∑h(1-h)=0 for binary; can ignore. c01[k]=∑? For full side max: c_pos[k] = ∑_{i=0}^{n-k-1} h_i*(1-h_{i+k}); c_neg[k] = ∑_{i=0}^{n-k-1} h_{i+k}*(1-h_i) maybe. With np.full, we can maintain both arrays length n for lags k=0..n-1. At initialization compute by loops O(n^2) (n≤1000 okay). Max C = dx * max(max(c_pos),max(c_neg)). For binary, c arrays ints. Objective integer max_count.
Flip pos p from old bit b to b^1:
For each lag k=1..n-1:
 - pair (p, p+k) if p+k<n: if old contributed to which count? Before flip, at lag k, term c_pos[k] includes if h[p]=1,h[p+k]=0. c_neg[k] includes if h[p+k]=1,h[p]=0. After flip, update.
 - pair (p-k, p) if p-k>=0: same lag k, term c_pos includes if h[p-k]=1,h[p]=0; c_neg includes if h[p]=1,h[p-k]=0.
But only pairs where one is p. For each k≤min(p, n-1-p?), both pairs maybe. We can update c_pos/k and c_neg/k using neighbor bit q.
Pseudo for old bit b, q = bits other:
For pair (u,v) with v=u+k (u<p<v? Actually if v=p and u=p-k):
   before_side_pos = (bits[u]==1 and bits[v]==0)
   before_side_neg = (bits[v]==1 and bits[u]==0)
   after flips p only.
But we can derive delta for c_pos[k] if p is first index u (v=p+k, q=bits[v]): term before= (b==1 and q==0); after=(1-b==1 and q==0) = (b==0 and q==0). delta = after-before.
If p is second index v (u=p-k, q=bits[u]): term before=(q==1 and b==0); after=(q==1 and 1-b==0) = (q==1 and b==0 -> 0 if b=1 else 0?) Wait after p flipped; if b=0, after b=1, term (q=1 and p=0)=q; before (q=1 and p=0)=q; no change? Let's carefully:
c_pos term for pair (u,v) = h_u*(1-h_v).
Case p is u (u=p, v=q): before b*(1-q); after (1-b)*(1-q). delta = (1-b-b)*(1-q) = (1-2b)(1-q).
Case p is v (u=q, v=p): before q*(1-b); after q*(1-(1-b))=q*b. delta = q*(b - (1-b)) = (2b-1)q.
Similarly c_neg term = h_v*(1-h_u).
Case p is u: before q*(1-b); after q*b: delta = q*(2b-1).
Case p is v: before b*(1-q); after (1-b)*(1-q): delta = (1-2b)(1-q).
So update with these deltas.

  global n, active indices into full corr? We can compute full corr for active update. For objective+grad, given h, compute vals for active full indices? But active indices are full array indices. g=corr* dx. To compute gradient of a corr value, need mapping from full index to lag. Numpy full index idx corresponds to lag k = idx - (n-1). For k=0, grad=1-2h. For k>0: c=corr[idx] =? Which side? Correlate full at idx = lag k where c_k = ∑ a_{m+k} b_m. For k>0: c = B_k? Because a=h,b=1-h -> h_{m+k}(1-h_m). A simpler local improvement: For current h and current C, take active lags A (those with f_l near C). def projected_gradient(h, n, beta, active, t0, budget, max_iter=5000):
    h=project_exact(h,n)
    best_local = compute_c5_direct(h)
    active = find_active(full_c(h), n, top=50, gap=0.005)
    prev_h=None; prev_g=None; bb=0.1
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time_left <5 break
       gval, grad = active_obj_grad(h, beta, active) # returns for smooth value (dx) and gradient
       gn = np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if gn <1e-12: break
       # If smooth value not decreasing? We'll accept based on exact C.
       # Determine step size
       if prev_h is not None:
          dh = h-prev_h; dg = grad-prev_g
          denom = np.dot(dg,dg)
          numer = np.dot(dh,dg)
          if denom>1e-20 and numer>0:
             bb = 0.5 * numer/denom? Barzilai-Borwein: step = ||xk-xk-1||^2/((xk-xk-1)·(gk-gk-1))
             numer2=np.dot(dh,dh); if >0: bb=numer2/denom
       alphas = [bb, 0.5*bb, 0.25*bb, 0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]
       # Clamp alpha to max step 2/normgrad? 
       accepted=False
       for a in alphas:
          a = float(np.clip(a, 1e-6, 1.0/(gn+1e-12))) # no huge
          trial = project_fast(h - a*grad,n)
          ct = compute_c5_direct(trial) # maybe expensive; use direct for n<=512 else FFT
          if ct < best_local - 1e-12:
              h=trial; best_local=ct; accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
          if it % 25 ==0 or sumct? update active from full c
          prev_h=h; prev_g=grad
       else:
          # try pure exact active average subgradient with smaller steps? Or perturb
          prev_h=h; prev_g=grad
          # maybe update active
          if it % 10 ==0:
             active=find_active(full_c(h),n,top=50,gap=0.01)
             beta = max(5,beta*1.2) maybe?
    return h,best_local

def obj_lbfgs(x): return smooth + lam*(sum-x)^2; grad = smooth_grad + 2*lam*(sum-target)*ones.

Implement side gradient functions for continuous but can use for binary:
def grad_side_P(x,k): (A lower-high)
  n=len; L=n-k; g=np.zeros(n)
  g[:L] += (1 - x[k:])
  g[k:] -= x[:L]
  return g
def grad_side_N(x,k): (B high-low)
  g=np.zeros(n)
  g[k:] += (1 - x[:L])
  g[:L] -= x[k:]
  return g
For k=0: g = 1-2x (but binary zero).

def obj_lbfgs_smooth(x):

FFT implementation:
def full_corr_fft(h):
  n=len; N=1
  while N < 2*n-1: N*=2
  fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N); fb=np.fft.rfft(1-h,N)
  c=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fb),N)
  # c_circ order lag 0,1,...,N-1. Need lags -(n-1)..n-1.
  return np.concatenate((c[N-(n-1):], c[:n]))
Does this match np.correlate? For max okay. Need check order and scaling. FFT correlation gives circular c[k]=∑ a[n+k] b[n] for k mod N; with zero padding, lags. The sequence c[0]=k=0, c[1]=k=1,..., c[N-(n-1)]=k=-(n-1). So full = [c[-(n-1)],...,c[-1],c[0],c[1],...,] i.e. concatenate c[N-(n-1):N] (lags -n+1..-1), c[0:n] (0..n-1). Good.
Floating errors may make values slightly negative? max okay. For exact direct use n<=700.

Active select function:
def select_active(full_vals, n, top_m=50, gap=0.005):
   c=full_vals*dx
   mx=c.max()
   idx=np.where(c >= mx - gap)[0]
   if len(idx)>top_m: idx=idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[-top_m:]]
   elif len(idx)<10: idx=np.argsort(c)[-top_m:]
   # ensure include argmax k=0? If c0 close? If c0 > mx-? It included.
   return idx

def projected_gradient(h,n,beta,active,t0,budget,max_iter=3000):
  c_best=eval
  prev_h=None; prev_g=None; step=0.1
  for it:
    vals, weights = active_values_weights(h,beta,active)
    g = grad ...
    # normalize gradient? 
    gn = np.linalg.norm(g)
    if gn<1e-12 break
    # Try step sizes: step, step/2, step/4, 0.01,0.005
    # Use BB if possible
    accepted=False
    for a in ...:
       trial=project_fast(h-a*g,n)
       ct=eval_c5(trial)
       if ct < c_best - 1e-12: accept
    if accepted update c_best, prev; step *=1.05 maybe
    else: update active; beta maybe; step*=0.5
  return

Binary global functions:
_global _bin_n, _bin_bits, _bin_cP, _bin_cN, _bin_lagL, _bin_lagR.
bin_init(bits):
  n=len(bits); _bin_n=n; _bin_bits=bits.astype(np.int8).copy()
  cP=np.zeros(n,np.int32); cN=...
  b=_bin_bits
  for k in range(1,n):
     L=n-k
     a=b[:L]; c=b[k:]
     cP[k] = int(np.dot(a, 1-c)) # a int8, 1-c int? For int8, 1-c okay.
     cN[k] = int(np.dot(c, 1-a))
  _bin_cP=cP; _bin_cN=cN
  lagsL=[]; lagsR=[]
  for p in range(n): lagsL.append(np.arange(1,p+1,dtype=np.intp) if p>0 else np.empty(0)); lagsR.append(np.arange(1,n-p,dtype=np.intp) if p+1<n else empty)
  _bin_max = int(max(np.max(cP), np.max(cN)))
bin_flip(p,b):
  bits=_bin_bits; cP=_bin_cP; cN=_bin_cN; n=_bin_n
  if b==1: a=-1; c=1
  else: a=1; c=-1
  # right lags
  lr=_bin_lagR[p]
  if lr.size:
     q = bits[p+1:]  # int8 view length n-p-1
     # deltas
     # cP[lr] += a*(1-q)
     # For q int8, 1-q returns int8? If q=0 ->1, q=1->0. okay.
     cP[lr] += a * (1 - q)
     cN[lr] += c * q
  ll=_bin_lagL[p]
  if ll.size:
     q = bits[p-1::-1]  # int8 view length p reversed (not contiguous but read)
     cP[ll] += c * q
     cN[ll] += a * (1 - q)
  bits[p] = 1 - b
Potential dtype: a,c Python int; 1-q int8; np int8 * int -> int8? If a=-1, product int8? Values -1,0; okay. Add to int32. But if q is int8, (1 - q) for q int8 gives int8? q=1 ->0, q=0 ->1; no overflow. Good. For q reversed view non-contiguous; operations produce temp.
Need ensure lr and ll are unique indices. Yes.
Check formulas with example h=[1,0], cP[1]=1? bits p=0 b=1, right q=[0], a=-1,c=1: cP += -1*(1-0)=-1? Initial cP[1]=1 ->0 after p becomes0? bits become [0,0], cP[1]=0 correct. cN[1] +=1*0=0. If flip p=0 b=0 from [0,0] to [1,0]: a=1,c=-1: cP+=1*(1-0)=1; correct. Good.
Left example bit p=1 old b=0, left q=bits[0]=0, a=1,c=-1: cP[1]+=-1*0=0; cN[1]+=1*(1-0)=1. Before bits [0,0], after [0,1], cN (high first low second) h1*(1-h0)=1*1=1 correct. Good.

bin_try_swap for candidate:
def bin_do_swap(p,q): # p is 1, q is 0, perform
   bin_flip(p,1)
   bin_flip(q,0)
def bin_undo_swap(p,q): # current swapped p=0,q=1
   bin_flip(q,1)
   bin_flip(p,0)

def bin_candidate_pairs(ones, zeros, temp, rng, score=None):
  cand=[]
  if score is not None:
     pvals = score[ones]; qvals=score[zeros]
     if len(pvals)>0:
        p_idx = np.argpartition(pvals, min(2,len-1))[:3]
        top_p = ones[p_idx]
     else top_p=[]
     if len(qvals)>0:
        q_idx = np.argpartition(-qvals, min(2,len-1))[:3]
        top_q = zeros[q_idx]
     else top_q=[]
     for p in top_p:
        for q in top_q:
           if p != q and _bin_bits[p]==1 and _bin_bits[q]==0: cand.append((int(p),int(q)))
  # random
  for _ in range(3):
     if ones and zeros:
        p=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]); q=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
        if p!=q: cand.append((p,q))
  # unique
  seen=set(); uniq=[]
  for pair in cand:
    if pair not in seen:
       if _bin_bits[pair[0]]==1 and _bin_bits[pair[1]]==0:
          uniq.append(pair); seen.add(pair)
  return uniq

project_exact(h,n):
  y=np.clip(h,0,1).copy(); target=n/2.0
  # bisection find shift t such that sum(clip(y-t,0,1))=target
  lo=float(np.min(y)-1.0); hi=float(np.max(y)+1.0)
  for _ in range(80):
    mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
    s=np.sum(np.clip(y-mid,0.0,1.0))
    if s>target: lo=mid
    else: hi=mid
  t=0.5*(lo+hi)
  h=np.clip(y-t,0,1)
  # adjust residual
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-14:
    # add to largest free in direction
    mask=(h>0)&(h<1)
    if mask.any():
       h[mask]+=diff/mask.sum(); h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    else:
       h+=diff/n; h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  return h
But bisection with y maybe not sorted; if target at bounds, works. Use 80 iter O(n*80). For many calls maybe heavy. Use fast for line search.

select_active(c,n,top_m=50,gap=0.005):
  dx=2/n; vals=c*dx; mx=vals.max(); order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1]
  idx=list(order[:top_m])
  # add near max not already
  near=np.where(vals >= mx-gap)[0]
  for i in near: if i not in idx: idx.append(i)
  if len(idx)>top_m*2: idx=order[:top_m].tolist()
  # if len<5 fill order
  while len(idx)<min(top_m,len(c)): add order[len]
  return np.array(idx,dtype=int)

Prepare active info:
def set_active(active,n,beta):
  global _g_n,_g_beta,_g_active,_g_info
  _g_n=n; _g_beta=float(beta); _g_active=active
  info=[]
  for idx in active:
     k=int(idx-(n-1))
     if k==0: info.append((0,1,0))
     elif k>0: L=n-k; info.append((k,L,1)) # type B
     else: q=-k; L=n-q; info.append((k,L,2)) # type A
  _g_info=info

Active values and LSE:
def active_value(x):
  n=_g_n; dx=2.0/n; m=len(_g_info); vals=np.empty(m)
  for i,info in enumerate(_g_info):
    k,L,typ=info
    if typ==0:
       vals[i]=np.dot(x,1.0-x)*dx
    elif typ==1: # k>0 B: x[k:]*(1-x[:L])
       vals[i]=np.dot(x[k:], 1.0-x[:L])*dx
    else: # k<0 A: x[:L]*(1-x[-L? q:]) q = -k = n-L? Since L=n-q -> q=n-L
       q=n-L
       vals[i]=np.dot(x[:L], 1.0-x[q:])*dx
  if _g_beta < 1e-8: return float(vals.max()) if m else 0.0, None
  b=_g_beta
  mx=vals.max()
  e=np.exp(b*(vals-mx))
  s=e.sum()
  return float(mx + np.log(s)/b), e/s

Gradient:
def active_grad(x,w):
  n=_g_n; g=np.zeros(n)
  for w_i,info in zip(w,_g_info):
    if w_i <= 1e-15: continue
    k,L,typ=info
    if typ==0: g += w_i*(1.0-2.0*x)
    elif typ==1:
       g[k:] += w_i*(1.0 - x[:L])
       g[:L] -= w_i*x[k:]
    else:
       q=n-L
       g[:L] += w_i*(1.0 - x[q:])
       g[q:] -= w_i*x[:L]
  return g

Potential issue: In value for typ=2 (k<0 A), L=n-q. x[q:] length L. Good.

SLSQP call:
def slsqp_once(h,n,beta,active,maxiter):
  global _g_n... set_active(active,n,beta)
  bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
  cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_con,'jac':sum_con_jac}]
  try:
    res=minimize(obj_active_smooth, h, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, jac=jac_active_smooth, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14,'iprint':0})
    return res.x
  except: return h

Projected gradient:
def projected_gradient(h,n,beta0,active, t0,budget, max_iter=2000):
  h=project_fast(h,n); c_best=eval_c5(h)[0]; beta=beta0; it_no=0
  prev_h=None; prev_g=None; step=0.05
  for it in range(max_iter):
    if time_left(t0,budget)<3: break
    vals,w=active_val_weight(h)
    g=active_grad(h,w)
    gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
    if gn<1e-13: break
    # compute BB
    if prev_h is not None:
       dh=h-prev_h; dg=g-prev_g; dd=float(np.dot(dh,dg)); gd=float(np.dot(g-prev_g,g-prev_g))?
    alphas=[step, step*0.5, step*0.25, 0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002]
    # cap max 1/gn
    accepted=False
    for a in alphas:
      if a<=0: continue
      maxa=0.5/(gn+1e-12)? Actually gradient of logsumexp scale dx? Could be O(1). Use 1.0.
      if a > 1.0/(gn+1e-12): a=1.0/(gn+1e-12)
      trial=project_fast(h-a*g,n)
      ct,_=eval_c5(trial)
      if ct < c_best - 1e-12:
         h=trial; c_best=ct; accepted=True; break
    if accepted:
      prev_h=h; prev_g=g; it_no=0; step=min(10.0, step*1.1)
    else:
      it_no +=1
      if it_no % 20 ==0:
         full=full_corr(h); active=select_active(full,n,top_m=50,gap=0.005)
         set_active(active,n,beta)
         beta=max(5.0,beta*1.3)
         step*=0.5
         if beta>5000: beta=5000
      else:
         set_active(active,n,beta)
         step*=0.7
    # If no improvement for long, perturb slightly
    if it_no > 80:
       h=project_fast(h + np.random.normal(0,0.01,n),n)
       c_best=eval_c5(h)[0]
       it_no=0; step=0.02
  return h,c_best

def optimize_continuous(h,n,t0,budget):
  best_h=project_exact(h,n); best_c=eval_c5(best_h)[0]
  # L-BFGS stages
  if time_left>30 and n<=600:
    for beta in [10,30,100,300]:
       if time_left<20 break
       full=full_corr(best_h); active=select_active(full,n,40,0.005); set_active(active,n,beta)
       for lam in [50,200,800]:
          global _g_lam; _g_lam=lam
          res=minimize(obj_lbfgs,best_h,...) ; project; update
  # SLSQP if n<=300
  if n<=300:
    betas = [20,80,300,1000,3000]
    for beta in betas:
      if time_left<40 break
      full=full_corr(best_h); top=50 if beta<300 else 15; gap=0.01 if beta<300 else 0.002
      active=select_active(...)
      for maxit in [200,400]:
         x=slsqp_once(...)
         h2=project_exact(x,n); c2=eval; update
  # projected gradient
  if time_left>60:
     full=full_corr(best_h); active=select_active(full,n,30,0.004); set_active
     for attempt in [0,1,2]:
        h2,c2=projected_gradient(... beta=300, active, max_iter based on time)
        update
  return best_h,best_c

```
import numpy as np
import time
```

time_left:
```
def time_left(t0,budget):
    return max(0.0, float(budget) - (time.time()-t0))
```

project_fast:
```
def project_fast(h,n):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float).copy()
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(40):
       diff=target - float(np.sum(h))
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1.0-1e-12)
       cnt=int(np.sum(mask))
       if cnt>0:
          h[mask] += diff/cnt
       else:
          h += diff/n
       h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    diff=target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       mask=(h>0.0)&(h<1.0)
       if mask.any(): h[mask]+=diff/np.sum(mask)
       h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    return h
```
project_exact as above.

safe_eval:
```
def eval_c5(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    n=len(h)
    if n==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
       return float('inf'), None
    dx=2.0/n
    c=full_corr(h)
    if c is None:
       return float('inf'), None
    return float(np.max(c)*dx), c
```
full_corr:
```
def full_corr(h):
   n=len(h)
   if n<=700:
      return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
   N=1
   while N < 2*n-1: N <<=1
   a=np.zeros(N); a[:n]=h
   b=np.zeros(N); b[:n]=1.0-h
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a); fb=np.fft.rfft(b)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fb), N)
   return np.concatenate((c[N-(n-1):], c[:n]))
```
We can avoid zero arrays by rfft with n=N: `fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N)`. Good.

interp_h:
```
def interp_h(src,src_n,dst_n):
   if src_n==dst_n: return project_exact(src,dst_n)
   xp=(np.arange(src_n)+0.5)*2.0/src_n
   xq=(np.arange(dst_n)+0.5)*2.0/dst_n
   v=np.interp(xq,xp,src)
   return project_exact(v,dst_n)
```

make_sine:
```
def make_sine_init(n,rng):
   t=np.arange(n)/float(n)
   ph=rng.rand()
   h=0.5+0.35*np.sin(2*np.pi*3.0*t+ph)+0.15*np.sin(2*np.pi*7.0*t+2.0*ph)+0.05*np.sin(2*np.pi*13*t+3*ph)
   return project_exact(h,n)
```

make_random_balanced:

```
def make_random_balanced(n,rng):
   idx=rng.choice(n,size=n//2,replace=False)
   h=np.zeros(n); h[idx]=1.0; return h
```
make_thue_morse and balance:
```
def make_thue_morse(n):
   h=np.zeros(n)
   # vectorized? for loop okay up to 1024
   for i in range(n):
      v=i; c=0
      while v: c ^= (v & 1); v >>=1
      h[i]=1.0 if c else 0.0
   return h
def balance_binary(h):
   n=len(h); target=n//2; s=int(np.sum(h))
   h=np.array(h,float).copy()
   if s>target:
      ones=np.where(h>=0.5)[0][:s-target]
      h[ones]=0.0
   elif s<target:
      zeros=np.where(h<0.5)[0][:target-s]
      h[zeros]=1.0
   return h
```

select_active:
```
def select_active(c,n,top_m=40,gap=0.005):
   if c is None: return np.arange(2*n-1)
   dx=2.0/n; vals=c*dx; order=np.argsort(vals)[::-1].astype(int)
   keep=[]
   seen=set()
   mx=float(vals[order[0]])
   for idx in order:
       if vals[idx] >= mx - gap:
           keep.append(int(idx)); seen.add(idx)
       if len(keep) >= top_m:
           break
   # fill with next top if not enough
   for idx in order:
       if len(keep) >= top_m: break
       idx=int(idx)
       if idx not in seen:
          keep.append(idx); seen.add(idx)
   if len(keep)==0: keep=[int(order[0])]
   return np.array(keep,dtype=int)
```
Maybe gap small; if no enough, fill top. Good.

set_active:
```
def set_active(active,n,beta):
   global _g_n,_g_beta,_g_active,_g_info
   _g_n=int(n); _g_beta=float(beta); _g_active=np.asarray(active,dtype=int)
   info=[]
   for idx in _g_active:
      k=int(idx) - (_g_n-1)
      if k==0: info.append((0,1,0))
      elif k>0: info.append((k,_g_n-k,1))
      else:
         q=-k; L=_g_n-q; info.append((k,L,2))
   _g_info=info
```

active_val_weight:
```
def active_val_weight(x):
   if _g_info is None: return 0.0, None
   m=len(_g_info)
   if m==0: return 0.0, None
   n=_g_n; dx=2.0/n
   vals=np.empty(m)
   for i,info in enumerate(_g_info):
      k,L,typ=info
      if typ==0:
         vals[i]=float(np.dot(x,1.0-x))
      elif typ==1:
         vals[i]=float(np.dot(x[k:],1.0-x[:L]))
      else:
         q=n-L
         vals[i]=float(np.dot(x[:L],1.0-x[q:]))
   vals *= dx
   b=_g_beta
   if b < 1e-6:
      mx=float(np.max(vals))
      w=np.zeros(m); w[int(np.argmax(vals))]=1.0
      return mx,w
   mx=float(np.max(vals))
   e=np.exp(b*(vals-mx))
   s=float(np.sum(e))
   if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
      w=np.zeros(m); w[int(np.argmax(vals))]=1.0; return mx,w
   return float(mx + np.log(s)/b), e/s
```

active_grad:
```
def active_grad(x,w):
   n=_g_n; g=np.zeros(n)
   for wi,info in zip(w,_g_info):
      if wi <= 1e-15: continue
      k,L,typ=info
      if typ==0:
         g += wi*(1.0-2.0*x)
      elif typ==1:
         g[k:] += wi*(1.0 - x[:L])
         g[:L] -= wi*x[k:]
      else:
         q=n-L
         g[:L] += wi*(1.0 - x[q:])
         g[q:] -= wi*x[:L]
   return g
```

obj/jac:
```
def obj_active_smooth(x):
    val,_=active_val_weight(x); return val
def jac_active_smooth(x):
    _,w=active_val_weight(x)
    if w is None: return np.zeros(_g_n)
    return active_grad(x,w)
def obj_lbfgs(x):
    val,_=active_val_weight(x)
    s=float(np.sum(x)-_g_n/2.0)
    return val + _g_lam*s*s
def jac_lbfgs(x):
    _,w=active_val_weight(x)
    if w is None: w=np.ones(len(_g_info))/max(1,len(_g_info))
    g=active_grad(x,w)
    s=float(np.sum(x)-_g_n/2.0)
    g += 2.0*_g_lam*s
    return g
def sum_constraint(x): return float(np.sum(x)-_g_n/2.0)
def sum_constraint_jac(x): return np.ones(_g_n)
```
For jac_lbfgs, adding scalar to array works: `g += 2.0*_g_lam*s` broadcasts. Good.

slsqp_once:

```
def slsqp_once(h,n,beta,active,maxiter):
    set_active(active,n,beta)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] for _ in range(n) # cannot use for with variable _? okay
```
Actually `[(0.0,1.0)] * n` okay.

```
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_constraint,'jac':sum_constraint_jac}]
    try:
       res=minimize(obj_active_smooth,h,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,jac=jac_active_smooth,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14,'iprint':0})
       x=res.x
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return h
       return x
    except Exception:
       return h
```

projected_gradient:
```
def projected_gradient(h,n,beta,active,t0,budget,max_iter=2000):
   set_active(active,n,beta)
   best_h=project_fast(h,n)
   best_c,_=eval_c5(best_h)
   prev_h=None; prev_g=None; step=0.05; no_imp=0; cur_beta=beta
   for it in range(max_iter):
      if time_left(t0,budget) < 3: break
      val,w=active_val_weight(best_h)
      g=active_grad(best_h,w)
      gn=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
      if gn < 1e-13: break
      alphas=[]
      if prev_h is not None:
         dh=best_h-prev_h; dg=g-prev_g
         num=float(np.dot(dh,dh)); den=float(np.dot(dg,dg))
         if den>1e-18 and num>0:
            bb=num/den
            alphas.append(bb)
            alphas.append(0.5*bb)
      alphas += [0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001]
      # replace with step? Maybe use step-based:
      # We'll include step candidates manually.
      alphas = list(dict.fromkeys([step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.1] + alphas))
      accepted=False
      for a in alphas:
         if a <= 0 or not np.isfinite(a): continue
         max_a = 1.0/(gn+1e-12)
         if a > max_a: a = max_a
         trial=project_fast(best_h - a*g, n)
         ct,_=eval_c5(trial)
         if ct < best_c - 1e-12:
             best_h=trial; best_c=ct; accepted=True; no_imp=0; step=min(5.0, step*1.2)
             break
      if not accepted:
         no_imp +=1
         step*=0.6
         if no_imp % 15 ==0:
            full,_=eval_c5(best_h)
            active=select_active(full,n,40,0.005)
            cur_beta = min(5000.0, cur_beta*1.25 + 10.0)
            set_active(active,n,cur_beta)
            if no_imp > 60:

               best_h=project_fast(best_h + np.random.normal(0.0,0.01,n), n)
               best_c,_=eval_c5(best_h)
               no_imp=0; step=0.02
      if accepted:
         prev_h=best_h; prev_g=g
      else:
         prev_h=best_h; prev_g=g
   return best_h,best_c
```

```
def optimize_continuous(h,n,t0,budget,rng,beta_limit=5000,max_iter=3000):
    global _g_lam
    best_h=project_exact(h,n)
    best_c,_=eval_c5(best_h)
    # if n small and active empty? 
    for beta in [10,30,100,300]:
       if time_left(t0,budget)<30: break
       _,full=eval_c5(best_h)
       active=select_active(full,n,40,0.005)
       set_active(active,n,beta)
       for lam in [50,200,800]:
          if time_left(t0,budget)<15: break
          _g_lam=lam
          res=maximize? use minimize obj_lbfgs with active set global; but active set unchanged. Need if active set set already.
          try:
             bounds=[(0,1)]*n
             res=minimize(obj_lbfgs,best_h,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,jac=jac_lbfgs,options={'maxiter':300,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':100})
             h2=project_exact(res.x,n); c2=eval; if < update
          except: pass
    # SLSQP
    if n <= 300:
       for beta in [20,80,300,1000,3000]:
          if time_left<40: break
          _,full=eval_c5(best_h)
          top=60 if beta<300 else 20
          gap=0.008 if beta<300 else 0.002
          active=select_active(full,n,top,gap)
          for maxit in [200,400]:
             if time_left<25: break
             x=slsqp_once(best_h,n,beta,active,maxit)
             h2=project_exact(x,n); c2=eval; update
    # projected gradient
    if time_left>60:
       _,full=eval_c5(best_h); active=select_active(full,n,40,0.005)
       for beta in [200,800,2000]:
          if time_left<30: break
          h2,c2=projected_gradient(best_h,n,beta,active,t0,budget,3000,rng)
          if c2<best_c: update
    return best_h,best_c
```

```
def try_shifts(h,n,t0,budget):
```

```
def bin_make_lists(bits):
   pos1={}; pos0={}
   ones=[]; zeros=[]
   for i,b in enumerate(bits):
      if b>=0.5: ones.append(i); pos1[i]=len(ones)-1
      else: zeros.append(i); pos0[i]=len(zeros)-1
   return ones,pos1,zeros,pos0
```

```
def bin_update_lists_swap(ones,pos1,zeros,pos0,p,q):
   ip=pos1[p]; iz=pos0[q]
   ones[ip]=q; pos1[q]=ip; del pos1[p]
   zeros[iz]=p; pos0[p]=iz; del pos0[q]
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_g_n = 0
_g_beta = 50.0
_g_active = None
_g_info = None
_g_lam = 100.0

_bin_n = 0
_bin_bits = None
_bin_cP = None
_bin_cN = None
_bin_lagL = None
_bin_lagR = None


def time_left(t0, budget):
    return max(0.0, float(budget) - (time.time() - t0))


def project_fast(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(40):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        mask = (h > 1e-12) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        cnt = int(np.sum(mask))
        if cnt > 0:
            h[mask] += diff / float(cnt)
        else:
            h += diff / float(n)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-11:
        mask = (h > 0.0) & (h < 1.0)
        if int(np.sum(mask)) > 0:
            h[mask] += diff / float(np.sum(mask))
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def project_exact(h, n):
    y = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    t = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    h = np.clip(y - t, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        mask = (h > 0.0) & (h < 1.0)
        if int(np.sum(mask)) > 0:
            h[mask] += diff / float(np.sum(mask))
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def full_corr(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 700:
        return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    need = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1
    while N < need:
        N <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(1.0 - h, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fb), N)
    return np.concatenate((c[N - (n - 1):], c[:n]))


def eval_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return float("inf"), None
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = full_corr(h)
    if c is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return float("inf"), None
    return float(np.max(c)) * dx, c


def interp_h(src, src_n, dst_n):
    src = np.asarray(src, dtype=float)
    if src_n == dst_n:
        return project_exact(src, dst_n)
    xp = (np.arange(src_n) + 0.5) * 2.0 / float(src_n)
    xq = (np.arange(dst_n) + 0.5) * 2.0 / float(dst_n)
    v = np.interp(xq, xp, src)
    return project_exact(v, dst_n)


def make_sine_init(n, rng):
    t = np.arange(n) / float(n)
    ph = rng.rand()
    h = (
        0.5
        + 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 3.0 * t + ph)
        + 0.15 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 7.0 * t + 2.0 * ph)
        + 0.05 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 13.0 * t + 3.0 * ph)
    )
    return project_exact(h, n)


def make_random_balanced(n, rng):
    idx = rng.choice(n, size=n // 2, replace=False)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    h[idx] = 1.0
    return h


def make_thue_morse(n):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        v = i
        c = 0
        while v:
            c ^= (v & 1)
            v >>= 1
        h[i] = 1.0 if c else 0.0
    return h


def balance_binary(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(h)
    target = n // 2
    s = int(np.sum(h))
    if s > target:
        ones = np.where(h >= 0.5)[0][: s - target]
        h[ones] = 0.0
    elif s < target:
        zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0][: target - s]
        h[zeros] = 1.0
    return h


def threshold_bits(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    bits = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1][: n // 2]
    bits[idx] = 1.0
    return bits


def select_active(c, n, top_m=40, gap=0.005):
    if c is None:
        return np.arange(2 * n - 1)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    vals = c * dx
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
        return np.arange(2 * n - 1)
    order = np.argsort(vals)[::-1].astype(int)
    mx = float(vals[order[0]])
    keep = []
    seen = set()
    for idx in order:
        if len(keep) >= top_m:
            break
        if vals[idx] >= mx - gap:
            keep.append(int(idx))
            seen.add(int(idx))
    for idx in order:
        if len(keep) >= top_m:
            break
        idx = int(idx)
        if idx not in seen:
            keep.append(idx)
            seen.add(idx)
    if not keep:
        keep = [int(order[0])]
    return np.array(keep, dtype=int)


def set_active(active, n, beta):
    global _g_n, _g_beta, _g_active, _g_info
    _g_n = int(n)
    _g_beta = float(beta)
    _g_active = np.asarray(active, dtype=int)
    info = []
    for idx in _g_active:
        k = int(idx) - (_g_n - 1)
        if k == 0:
            info.append((0, 1, 0))
        elif k > 0:
            info.append((k, _g_n - k, 1))
        else:
            q = -k
            L = _g_n - q
            info.append((k, L, 2))
    _g_info = info


def active_val_weight(x):
    if _g_info is None:
        return 0.0, None
    m = len(_g_info)
    if m == 0:
        return 0.0, None
    n = _g_n
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    vals = np.empty(m, dtype=float)
    for i, info in enumerate(_g_info):
        k, L, typ = info
        if typ == 0:
            vals[i] = float(np.dot(x, 1.0 - x))
        elif typ == 1:
            vals[i] = float(np.dot(x[k:], 1.0 - x[:L]))
        else:
            q = n - L
            vals[i] = float(np.dot(x[:L], 1.0 - x[q:]))
    vals *= dx
    b = _g_beta
    if b < 1e-6:
        mx = float(np.max(vals))
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
        return mx, w
    mx = float(np.max(vals))
    e = np.exp(b * (vals - mx))
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
        return mx, w
    return float(mx + np.log(s) / b), e / s


def active_grad(x, w):
    n = _g_n
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for wi, info in zip(w, _g_info):
        if wi <= 1e-15:
            continue
        k, L, typ = info
        if typ == 0:
            g += wi * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
        elif typ == 1:
            g[k:] += wi * (1.0 - x[:L])
            g[:L] -= wi * x[k:]
        else:
            q = n - L
            g[:L] += wi * (1.0 - x[q:])
            g[q:] -= wi * x[:L]
    return g


def obj_active_smooth(x):
    v, _ = active_val_weight(x)
    return v


def jac_active_smooth(x):
    _, w = active_val_weight(x)
    if w is None:
        return np.zeros(_g_n)
    return active_grad(x, w)


def obj_lbfgs(x):
    v, _ = active_val_weight(x)
    s = float(np.sum(x) - _g_n / 2.0)
    return v + _g_lam * s * s


def jac_lbfgs(x):
    _, w = active_val_weight(x)
    if w is None:
        m = len(_g_info) if _g_info is not None else 1
        w = np.ones(m) / float(max(1, m))
    g = active_grad(x, w)
    s = float(np.sum(x) - _g_n / 2.0)
    g += 2.0 * _g_lam * s
    return g


def sum_constraint(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - _g_n / 2.0)


def sum_constraint_jac(x):
    return np.ones(_g_n)


def slsqp_once(h, n, beta, active, maxiter):
    set_active(active, n, beta)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_constraint, "jac": sum_constraint_jac}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            obj_active_smooth,
            np.asarray(h, dtype=float),
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            jac=jac_active_smooth,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-14, "iprint": 0},
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
        return x
    except Exception:
        return np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()


def projected_gradient(h, n, beta, active, deadline, rng):
    global _g_lam
    set_active(active, n, beta)
    best_h = project_fast(h, n)
    best_c, _ = eval_c5(best_h)
    prev_h = None
    prev_g = None
    step = 0.05
    no_imp = 0
    cur_beta = beta
    for _ in range(6000):
        if time.time() > deadline - 3.0:
            break
        _, w = active_val_weight(best_h)
        if w is None:
            break
        g = active_grad(best_h, w)
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gn < 1e-13:
            break
        raw = [step, step * 0.5, step * 0.25, step * 0.1, 0.1, 0.05,
               0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001]
        if prev_h is not None:
            dh = best_h - prev_h
            dg = g - prev_g
            num = float(np.dot(dh, dh))
            den = float(np.dot(dg, dg))
            if den > 1e-18 and num > 0.0:
                bb = num / den
                raw = [bb, 0.5 * bb] + raw
        raw = [float(a) for a in raw if np.isfinite(a) and a > 0.0]
        accepted = False
        max_a = 1.0 / (gn + 1e-12)
        for a in raw:
            if a > max_a:
                a = max_a
            trial = project_fast(best_h - a * g, n)
            ct, _ = eval_c5(trial)
            if ct < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_h = trial
                best_c = ct
                accepted = True
                no_imp = 0
                step = min(5.0, step * 1.2)
                break
        if accepted:
            prev_h = best_h.copy()
            prev_g = g.copy()
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            step *= 0.6
            prev_h = best_h.copy()
            prev_g = g.copy()
            if no_imp % 12 == 0:
                _, full = eval_c5(best_h)
                active = select_active(full, n, 40, 0.005)
                cur_beta = min(5000.0, cur_beta * 1.25 + 10.0)
                set_active(active, n, cur_beta)
            if no_imp > 60:
                best_h = project_fast(
                    best_h + 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(n), n
                )
                best_c, _ = eval_c5(best_h)
                _, full = eval_c5(best_h)
                active = select_active(full, n, 40, 0.005)
                set_active(active, n, cur_beta)
                no_imp = 0
                step = 0.02
    return best_h, best_c


def optimize_continuous(h, n, deadline, rng):
    global _g_lam
    best_h = project_exact(h, n)
    best_c, _ = eval_c5(best_h)

    if time.time() > deadline - 30.0:
        return best_h, best_c

    for beta in [10.0, 30.0, 100.0, 300.0]:
        if time.time() > deadline - 30.0:
            break
        _, full = eval_c5(best_h)
        active = select_active(full, n, 40, 0.005)
        set_active(active, n, beta)
        for lam in [50.0, 200.0, 800.0]:
            if time.time() > deadline - 15.0:
                break
            _g_lam = lam
            try:
                bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
                res = minimize(
                    obj_lbfgs,
                    best_h,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    jac=jac_lbfgs,
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 350,
                        "ftol": 1e-15,
                        "gtol": 1e-12,
                        "maxls": 100,
                    },
                )
                x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
                if np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
                    h2 = project_fast(x, n)
                    c2, _ = eval_c5(h2)
                    if c2 < best_c:
                        best_h = h2
                        best_c = c2
            except Exception:
                pass

    if n <= 300:
        for beta in [20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0]:
            if time.time() > deadline - 40.0:
                break
            _, full = eval_c5(best_h)
            if beta < 300.0:
                top = 60
                gap = 0.008
            else:
                top = 20
                gap = 0.0015
            active = select_active(full, n, top, gap)
            for maxit in [180, 320]:
                if time.time() > deadline - 25.0:
                    break
                x = slsqp_once(best_h, n, beta, active, maxit)
                h2 = project_fast(x, n)
                c2, _ = eval_c5(h2)
                if c2 < best_c:
                    best_h = h2
                    best_c = c2

    if time.time() > deadline - 60.0:
        return best_h, best_c

    _, full = eval_c5(best_h)
    active = select_active(full, n, 40, 0.005)
    for beta in [200.0, 800.0, 2000.0]:
        if time.time() > deadline - 30.0:
            break
        h2, c2 = projected_gradient(best_h, n, beta, active, deadline, rng)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    return best_h, best_c


def try_shifts(h, n, deadline):
    best = project_exact(h, n)
    best_c, _ = eval_c5(best)
    if n > 1:
        if n <= 350:
            shifts = range(1, n)
        else:
            shifts = range(1, n, max(1, n // 300))
        for r in shifts:
            if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
                break
            hn = np.roll(best, r)
            ct, _ = eval_c5(hn)
            if ct < best_c - 1e-12:
                best = hn
                best_c = ct
    hr = best[::-1].copy()
    ct, _ = eval_c5(hr)
    if ct < best_c - 1e-12:
        best = hr
        best_c = ct
    return best, best_c


def bin_init(bits):
    global _bin_n, _bin_bits, _bin_cP, _bin_cN, _bin_lagL, _bin_lagR
    n = len(bits)
    _bin_n = int(n)
    _bin_bits = np.asarray(bits, dtype=np.int8).copy()
    cP = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    cN = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    b = _bin_bits
    for k in range(1, n):
        L = n - k
        a = b[:L]
        c = b[k:]
        cP[k] = int(np.dot(a, 1 - c))
        cN[k] = int(np.dot(c, 1 - a))
    _bin_cP = cP
    _bin_cN = cN
    lagL = []
    lagR = []
    for p in range(n):
        lagL.append(np.arange(1, p + 1, dtype=np.intp) if p > 0 else np.empty(0, dtype=np.intp))
        lagR.append(np.arange(1, n - p, dtype=np.intp) if p + 1 < n else np.empty(0, dtype=np.intp))
    _bin_lagL = lagL
    _bin_lagR = lagR


def bin_recompute():
    global _bin_cP, _bin_cN
    n = _bin_n
    cP = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    cN = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    b = _bin_bits
    for k in range(1, n):
        L = n - k
        a = b[:L]
        c = b[k:]
        cP[k] = int(np.dot(a, 1 - c))
        cN[k] = int(np.dot(c, 1 - a))
    _bin_cP = cP
    _bin_cN = cN


def bin_flip(p, b):
    bits = _bin_bits
    cP = _bin_cP
    cN = _bin_cN
    if b == 1:
        a = -1
        c = 1
    else:
        a = 1
        c = -1
    lr = _bin_lagR[p]
    if lr.size:
        q = bits[p + 1:]
        cP[lr] += a * (1 - q)
        cN[lr] += c * q
    ll = _bin_lagL[p]
    if ll.size:
        q = bits[p - 1::-1]
        cP[ll] += c * q
        cN[ll] += a * (1 - q)
    bits[p] = np.int8(1 - b)


def grad_side_A(x, k):
    n = len(x)
    L = n - k
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    g[:L] += 1.0 - x[k:]
    g[k:] -= x[:L]
    return g


def grad_side_B(x, k):
    n = len(x)
    L = n - k
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    g[k:] += 1.0 - x[:L]
    g[:L] -= x[k:]
    return g


def bin_guided_score():
    n = _bin_n
    b = np.asarray(_bin_bits, dtype=float)
    maxC = int(max(np.max(_bin_cP), np.max(_bin_cN)))
    thresh = maxC - max(1, maxC // 20)
    items = []
    for k in range(1, n):
        v = int(_bin_cP[k])
        if v >= thresh:
            items.append((v, k, 0))
        v = int(_bin_cN[k])
        if v >= thresh:
            items.append((v, k, 1))
    if not items:
        for k in range(1, n):
            items.append((int(_bin_cP[k]), k, 0))
            items.append((int(_bin_cN[k]), k, 1))
        items.sort(reverse=True)
        items = items[:20]
    else:
        if len(items) > 35:
            items.sort(reverse=True)
            items = items[:35]
    m = len(items)
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    inv = 1.0 / float(m)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for _, k, typ in items:
        if typ == 0:
            g += inv * grad_side_A(b, k)
        else:
            g += inv * grad_side_B(b, k)
    return g


def bin_candidates(ones, zeros, score, rng):
    cand = []
    if score is not None and ones and zeros:
        pvals = score[ones]
        qvals = score[zeros]
        pm = min(3, len(pvals))
        qm = min(3, len(qvals))
        if pm > 0:
            pord = np.argpartition(pvals, pm - 1)[:pm]
        else:
            pord = []
        if qm > 0:
            qord = np.argpartition(-qvals, qm - 1)[:qm]
        else:
            qord = []
        for pi in pord:
            p = int(ones[pi])
            for qi in qord:
                q = int(zeros[qi])
                if p != q and _bin_bits[p] == 1 and _bin_bits[q] == 0:
                    cand.append((p, q))
    for _ in range(5):
        if ones and zeros:
            p = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
            q = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
            if p != q and _bin_bits[p] == 1 and _bin_bits[q] == 0:
                cand.append((p, q))
    uniq = []
    seen = set()
    for p, q in cand:
        key = (p, q)
        if key in seen:
            continue
        if _bin_bits[p] == 1 and _bin_bits[q] == 0:
            uniq.append(key)
            seen.add(key)
    return uniq


def bin_make_lists(bits):
    pos1 = {}
    pos0 = {}
    ones = []
    zeros = []
    for i, b in enumerate(bits):
        if b >= 0.5:
            pos1[i] = len(ones)
            ones.append(i)
        else:
            pos0[i] = len(zeros)
            zeros.append(i)
    return ones, pos1, zeros, pos0


def bin_apply_swap(ones, pos1, zeros, pos0, p, q):
    ip = pos1.get(p)
    iz = pos0.get(q)
    if ip is None or iz is None:
        return False
    ones[ip] = q
    pos1[q] = ip
    del pos1[p]
    zeros[iz] = p
    pos0[p] = iz
    del pos0[q]
    return True


def binary_search(bits, n, deadline, rng):
    global _bin_n, _bin_bits, _bin_cP, _bin_cN
    bits = balance_binary(np.asarray(bits, dtype=float))[:]
    bin_init(bits)
    best_bits = np.asarray(_bin_bits, dtype=float).copy()
    best_max = int(max(np.max(_bin_cP), np.max(_bin_cN)))
    best_c = best_max * 2.0 / float(n)
    cur_max = best_max
    ones, pos1, zeros, pos0 = bin_make_lists(_bin_bits)
    temp = max(0.2, 0.05 * float(best_max))
    no_imp = 0
    it = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 1.0:
        it += 1
        if not ones or not zeros:
            break
        if it % 20000 == 0:
            bin_recompute()
            cur_max = int(max(np.max(_bin_cP), np.max(_bin_cN)))
        score = bin_guided_score()
        cand = bin_candidates(ones, zeros, score, rng)
        improved = False
        for p, q in cand:
            if _bin_bits[p] != 1 or _bin_bits[q] != 0 or p == q:
                continue
            bin_flip(p, 1)
            bin_flip(q, 0)
            nm = int(max(np.max(_bin_cP), np.max(_bin_cN)))
            delta = nm - cur_max
            accept = False
            if delta < 0:
                accept = True
            elif temp > 0.05 and delta < 50.0 * temp:
                if rng.random() < float(np.exp(-delta / temp)):
                    accept = True
            if accept:
                cur_max = nm
                if cur_max < best_max:
                    best_max = cur_max
                    best_c = best_max * 2.0 / float(n)
                    best_bits = np.asarray(_bin_bits, dtype=float).copy()
                if not bin_apply_swap(ones, pos1, zeros, pos0, p, q):
                    ones, pos1, zeros, pos0 = bin_make_lists(_bin_bits)
                no_imp = 0
                improved = True
                break
            else:
                bin_flip(q, 1)
                bin_flip(p, 0)
        if not improved:
            no_imp += 1
            temp = max(0.05, temp * 0.995)
            if no_imp > 120:
                temp = min(3.0, temp + 0.3)
            if no_imp > 600:
                start = best_bits.copy()
                ones, pos1, zeros, pos0 = bin_make_lists(start)
                for _ in range(max(1, n // 20)):
                    if ones and zeros:
                        pp = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
                        qq = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
                        if pp == qq:
                            continue
                        bin_flip_state = None
                        if bin_apply_swap(ones, pos1, zeros, pos0, pp, qq):
                            pass
                bin_init(start)
                ones, pos1, zeros, pos0 = bin_make_lists(_bin_bits)
                cur_max = int(max(np.max(_bin_cP), np.max(_bin_cN)))
                no_imp = 0
    return best_bits, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    total_dead = t0 + budget
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    try:
        parent_h = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if len(parent_h) > 0:
            best_n = int(len(parent_h))
            best_h = project_exact(parent_h, best_n)
            best_c, _ = eval_c5(best_h)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_h is None:
        best_n = 160
        best_h = make_sine_init(best_n, rng)
        best_c, _ = eval_c5(best_h)

    # Resolution sweep from the current construction.
    res_dead = t0 + max(20.0, min(160.0, 0.10 * budget))
    if best_c < float("inf"):
        ns_set = set()
        for f in [0.5, 0.65, 0.8, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0]:
            nt = int(round(best_n * f))
            if nt % 2 == 1:
                nt += 1
            if 40 <= nt <= 1024:
                ns_set.add(nt)
        for m in [64, 80, 96, 120, 160, 192, 240, 320, 400, 512]:
            ns_set.add(m)
        ns = sorted(ns_set)
        for nt in ns:
            if time.time() > res_dead - 3.0:
                break
            if nt == best_n:
                continue
            try:
                ht = interp_h(best_h, best_n, nt)
                ct, _ = eval_c5(ht)
                if ct < best_c:
                    best_h = ht
                    best_c = ct
                    best_n = nt
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.time() > res_dead - 10.0:
        best_h, best_c = try_shifts(best_h, best_n, res_dead)

    # Main continuous optimization.
    cont_dead = min(total_dead - 260.0, t0 + max(260.0, 0.52 * budget))
    if time.time() < cont_dead - 20.0:
        try:
            best_h, best_c = optimize_continuous(best_h, best_n, cont_dead, rng)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.time() < cont_dead - 5.0:
        try:
            best_h, best_c = try_shifts(best_h, best_n, cont_dead)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Binary-inspired discrete search at a moderate even resolution.
    bin_dead = min(total_dead - 130.0, t0 + max(220.0, 0.82 * budget))
    if time.time() < bin_dead - 30.0:
        n_even = int(best_n)
        if n_even % 2 == 1:
            n_even += 1
        if n_even < 64:
            n_even = 64
        if n_even > 336:
            n_even = 320
        try:
            hn = interp_h(best_h, best_n, n_even)
            starts = []
            bits0 = threshold_bits(hn, n_even)
            starts.append(bits0)
            starts.append(make_random_balanced(n_even, rng))
            starts.append(balance_binary(make_thue_morse(n_even)))
            starts.append(balance_binary(np.clip(
                0.5 + 0.45 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * 5.0 * np.arange(n_even) / float(n_even)) + 0.15 * rng.standard_normal(n_even),
                0.0, 1.0
            )) * rng.rand(n_even))
            start_bits = None
            start_c = float("inf")
            for sb in starts:
                sbf = balance_binary(np.asarray(sb, dtype=float))
                sc, _ = eval_c5(sbf)
                if sc < start_c:
                    start_c = sc
                    start_bits = sbf
            if start_bits is not None and start_c < best_c:
                best_h = start_bits
                best_c = start_c
                best_n = n_even
            bb, bc = binary_search(start_bits if start_bits is not None else bits0,
                                   n_even, bin_dead, rng)
            if bc < best_c:
                best_h = bb
                best_c = bc
                best_n = n_even
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.time() < bin_dead - 20.0:
        try:
            best_h, best_c = try_shifts(best_h, best_n, bin_dead)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final continuous polish with the largest remaining time.
    final_dead = total_dead - 25.0
    if time.time() < final_dead - 60.0:
        try:
            best_h, best_c = optimize_continuous(best_h, best_n, final_dead - 25.0, rng)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.time() < final_dead - 12.0:
        try:
            best_h, best_c = try_shifts(best_h, best_n, final_dead - 12.0)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Ensure feasibility and final exact evaluation.
    best_h = project_exact(best_h, best_n)
    final_c5, _ = eval_c5(best_h)

    try:
        ec5 = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        if ec5 is not None and float(ec5) < final_c5:
            final_c5 = float(ec5)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(best_h)):
        best_h = make_sine_init(160, rng)
        final_c5, _ = eval_c5(best_h)

    return (best_h, float(final_c5), int(best_n))
```
